Review findings

删去了亚比米勒是耶路巴力之子的身份信息，以及他宣称自己与受话者有骨肉关系的独立断言，均属实质内容。编辑仅删除所列片段。全文仍可成立：“要记得。”可以省略宾语，承接前面的询问指示，并非必然构成悬空补语；但它如今究竟提醒记住什么不够明确，因此标记 ambiguous_reference。提醒对象也可能从骨肉关系转为前面的询问指示，因此标记 possible_other_meaning_changed。这种语义变化不单独导致上下文有效性失败。

Review findings

The edits remove Abimelech’s identification as Jerubbaal’s son and the instruction to question all the leaders of Shechem. Both are substantive source information. The complete edited window remains grammatical: the retained inner quotation encloses a complete question, and no attribution or complement is left dangling. Only the listed spans were deleted. The omitted instruction changes the apparent audience and function of the question: it now reads as addressed directly to the maternal relatives rather than as a question they should put to Shechem’s leaders. This semantic change is nonblocking; the omissions are supported by the bilingual source.

9:1　耶路巴力的儿子亚比米勒来到示剑，去见他母亲的兄弟们，对他们和他母亲的全族说：

9:2　“请你们问示剑所有的首领：‘是让耶路巴力的七十个儿子全都统治你们好，还是让一个人统治你们好？’要记得，我是你们的骨肉。”

Review findings

The omissions remove Abimelech’s parentage, his mother’s whole clan as additional addressees, and the explicit assertion of kinship. The final “要记得。” can naturally take the preceding request to ask the leaders as its implicit object, so it need not be an ungrammatical dangling complement. This nevertheless permits a different interpretation of what must be remembered. Kinship with the remaining addressees is recoverable from their identification as his mother’s brothers, although its explicit use in his appeal is lost. Only the documented spans are deleted.

recoverable_from_context, possible_other_meaning_changed

Clean-source answer key

B. 他母亲的兄弟和他母亲的全族

The key is for the clean passage. Defects can remove or alter its supporting evidence; this is not a separate answerability judgment for each defective passage.

The edits remove Zebul’s anger and the specified location of the ambush, both substantive source information. The remaining narration is grammatical, and “埋伏” can stand without a location complement. The result matches the listed deletions and remains supported by the bilingual window.

合并删除使跟随亚比米勒共同冲锋、占据城门口的队伍，以及另外两队冲向田间所有人的行动和明确目标范围均不再表达。“亚比米勒向前冲去，占据城门口；击杀他们”语法完整：分号后的省略主语可承接亚比米勒，宾语可承接上节的百姓。但这也使原由另外两队实施的击杀自然变成亚比米勒的行动，属于非阻断性的行动者变化。全文其余衔接成立，修改仅为所列删除，删失事实有双语原文支持。

删去了求问上帝这一具体要求，以及祭司让他们平平安安地去的指示，均属实质信息。整段删后中文自然、语法完整，且仅删除了所列片段。保留的“请你看看”不再明确要求借求问上帝来判断行程，而成为泛指查看或判断的请求，因此标记语义变化；这不影响上下文有效性。

18:15　他们就转到那里，来到那年轻利未人的住处，也就是米迦的家，向他问安。

  1. 他们没有与邻居结盟。
  2. 他们与邻居有密切的贸易往来。
  3. 他们与西顿结盟，互相保护。
  4. 他们与邻近城镇经常交战。

18:28　没有人来救他们，因为那城离西顿很远，也没有与任何人结盟；那城位于伯利合附近的山谷中。但人重建了那城，住在那里。

Review findings

删去了对当地人民的明确引入，以及他们平静、毫无戒备的状态；这些是实质性的受害者信息，而非仅省略可隐含的宾语。句法仍成立，但“用刀杀了他们”中的“他们”失去了明确的复数先行词，需要从拉亿推知当地居民，因此标记指代歧义。仅删除列明片段，所删内容与原文一致。

6:25　于是撒马利亚发生了大饥荒。他们围城日久，以致一个驴头卖到八十舍客勒银子，四分之一卡布的鸽子粪卖到五舍客勒银子。

Review findings

删除了召集全军、长期围城，以及鸽子粪价格所对应的四分之一卡布数量，均有实质信息损失。全文仍通顺，“鸽子粪卖到五舍客勒银子”可以成立，但价格对应的数量已不明确。此外，“大饥荒，以致……”把高价直接接为饥荒的结果，改变了原文明确表达的因果衔接；这些变化以语义变化标记，不构成语法或上下文有效性失败。所有改动均为所列删除。

  4. 回到帐篷里寻找食物。
  5. 无法判断
Removed text and unit counts

{'clause': 2, 'word': 1, 'phrase': 2}

7:8　那几个患麻风病的人到了营地边缘，进入一顶帐篷，又吃又喝。随后，他们拿走银子、金子和衣服，去藏了起来。回来后，他们又进了另一顶帐篷，从那里拿走一些东西，也藏了起来。

7:9　最后，他们彼此说：“我们这样做不对。今天是个有好消息的日子。如果我们不作声，等到天亮，我们的罪必追上我们。现在，我们去向王宫报信吧。”

Review findings

The combined edit remains grammatical, with permissible implicit subjects, and matches the listed deletions without additions or reordering. It removes substantive information: the participants are lepers, they reach the camp’s edge, the second tent is another tent, they explicitly acknowledge wrongdoing, and their intended reporting destination is the king’s household. These losses are supported by the bilingual source. ambiguous_reference: “他们” remains without an explicit identification of the participants in this window, although the continuing group is understandable. recoverable_from_context: the location expressed by “从那里” remains implicit in entering the tent and taking items; that deletion alone does not establish information loss. possible_other_meaning_changed: “又进了帐篷” can suggest returning to the same tent rather than entering another one. This possible semantic shift does not invalidate the omission in context.

13:24　他上路后，有一头狮子在路上遇见他，将他咬死。他的尸体倒在路上，驴和狮子都站在尸体旁边。

13:25　有人经过，看见尸体倒在路上，狮子站在尸体旁边，就到老先知所住的城里报告了这件事。

Review findings

The combined deletions remove the old prophet’s having brought the man of God back, his saddling the donkey for him, and the witnesses’ going to report in the city where the prophet lived. These are substantive relationships, actions, and location details explicitly supported by both source texts. The entire edited window remains grammatical and natural; 他 still refers clearly to 神人. The edits consist only of the listed deletions, with the original sentence-final punctuation retained.

42. 2 Kings 11:1–3

5% omission

4.8% actual · 6/124 source characters · Pass

11:1　亚哈谢的母亲亚他利雅见儿子死了，就着手灭绝所有王室后裔。

11:2　但亚哈谢的姐妹约示巴，将亚哈谢的儿子约阿施从那些正遭杀害的王子中偷偷带走。她把他和他的乳母藏在一间卧房里，躲避亚他利雅，使他没有被杀。

11:3　亚他利雅统治那地期间，约阿施和他的乳母一同藏在耶和华的殿里六年。

删去了乌西雅拥有随时备战军队的明确陈述、书记耶利和官员玛西雅登记军队编制的职责，以及长矛等多种装备和城楼这一器械设置地点，均属实质信息。全文仍可按中文省略主语的叙述理解：“按编定的队伍出征。受王的一位官员哈拿尼雅指挥。”没有悬空补语，但开头两句的行动主体不再明确，故标记 ambiguous_reference；后文的“战士”“军队”“全军”可帮助恢复军队这一主体，却不能恢复备战状态、登记人员等信息，故标记 recoverable_from_context。删去登记句后，哈拿尼雅的指挥更容易被理解为直接指挥出征，而非与编制登记相连，故标记 possible_other_meaning_changed；这不构成语法或语境有效性失败。编辑仅删除所列原文片段。

ambiguous_reference, recoverable_from_context, possible_other_meaning_changed

Clean-source answer key

C. 宗族首领

The key is for the clean passage. Defects can remove or alter its supporting evidence; this is not a separate answerability judgment for each defective passage.

The edits remove the specified location of the incense offering, “在香坛上,” and the substantive instruction to leave the sanctuary. Both omissions are supported by the bilingual source and match the listed deletions. The remaining sentence “因为你行了不忠的事；你不会因此得到耶和华上帝的尊荣。” can function as a causal compound sentence: the semicolon is marked but does not leave the causal clause without a main clause. The causal attachment changes, however: the stated unfaithfulness now directly explains the denial of honor rather than the deleted command to leave.

56. Acts 19:11–12

5% omission

3.8% actual · 2/52 source characters · Pass with flags

19:11　神借着保罗的手施行了非凡的神迹，

19:12　甚至有人把接触过他的手帕和围裙拿到那里，他们的疾病就好了，邪灵也离开了他们。

当接触过保罗的手帕和围裙被带到病人那里时，什么离开了他们？

  1. 神。
  2. 邪灵。
  3. 保罗。
  4. 送手帕和围裙的人。
  5. 无法判断
Removed text and unit counts

{'word': 1}

19:11　神借着保罗的手施行了非凡的神迹，

19:12　甚至有人把接触过他的手帕和围裙拿到病人那里，他们的疾病就好了，邪灵也离开了他们。

Review findings

Deleting “病人” removes the explicit identification of the sick as the recipients and destination of the items, a substantive relationship supported by both source texts. The edited window remains grammatical: “那里” and “他们” can function as context-dependent references, but their intended referents are no longer clearly established (ambiguous_reference). Readers might consequently associate the healing and deliverance with the people carrying the items rather than their recipients (possible_other_meaning_changed). This semantic uncertainty does not itself invalidate the omission. Only the listed deletion was made.

ambiguous_reference, possible_other_meaning_changed

Review findings

The combined edit removes the event becoming known to all the Jews and Greeks living in Ephesus, as well as the explicit statement that the practitioners owned the books they brought. 把书带来 remains grammatical, and the following 这些书 retains a clear antecedent. However, 众人 loses its original explicit referent, leaving the crowd ambiguous and potentially broadening its apparent scope. Both deletions match the listed spans, and the full edited window remains fluent.

Review findings

删去了要求听者与公议会一起向千夫长提出请求的指示，以及准备在路上杀死保罗的地点信息，均属实质内容。合并删节后，整段仍可自然地读作连续指令，语法和标点完整，且仅删除了所列文字。ambiguous_reference：千夫长不再出现，使“让他把保罗带到你们这里来”中的“他”缺少明确的指代对象。other_meaning_changed：原本通过向千夫长请愿实施的安排，变成直接要求听者假称审案并让某人带来保罗，请求的参与者与实施关系因此改变。这些语义变化不构成语法失败；删去的信息均有双语原文依据。

23:22　于是千夫长让那年轻人离开，并嘱咐他说：“不要告诉任何人你向我报告了这件事。”

Review findings

The edit removes the Sanhedrin destination, the investigative pretext, the commander’s dismissal of the young man, the explicit attribution of the instruction to the commander, and the report’s specified recipient. “把保罗带去” is grammatical without a stated destination. Likewise, “并嘱咐他说” can grammatically continue “他回答说”, although this makes the attribution ambiguous and favors the young man as instructor, changing the apparent roles. Removing “向我” broadens the secrecy instruction; the original report recipient remains recoverable from the conversation. These are substantive, source-supported omissions, and the exact edits contain only the listed deletions.

In “把保罗带到他面前”, deleting the governor's introduction makes “他” initially unclear, hence ambiguous_reference; the following sentence supplies the intended governor, hence recoverable_from_context. The accusers' connection to Paul also remains inferable from his case. These reference issues do not make the sentences ungrammatical. All changes match the listed deletions.

recoverable_from_context, ambiguous_reference

Review findings

The omissions remove the arrival in Caesarea, the presentation of Paul to the governor, and the governor’s question about Paul’s province. These are substantive facts supported by the bilingual original. The entire edited window remains grammatical: “把信交给总督。” permits an implicit subject, and “得知他来自基利家，就说……” remains intact across the verse boundary. However, deleting the explicit horsemen subject makes the letter-deliverer less clear after a sentence mentioning both infantry and horsemen (ambiguous_reference), potentially changing the apparent actor (possible_other_meaning_changed). “控告人” remains natural, with its relationship to Paul inferable from the surrounding case discussion. The edits match the listed deletions without additional changes.

ambiguous_reference, possible_other_meaning_changed
